> **GENERATED — do not edit by hand, and do not commit.** `bin/verification-map-build.sh` builds this file from
> `docs/verification-map/index/head.md`, `docs/verification-map/index/tail.md` and `docs/verification-map/entries/<TICKET-KEY>.md`.
> A build writes its own `entries/<TICKET-KEY>.md` and commits THAT; this index is git-ignored (BH-1175).

# Verification map — cromford

cromford has no user-facing app. Its "features" are cron lanes and shell
libraries on the hub and the fleet boxes, so an entry here answers the operator's
version of the same two questions the template asks: **how do you reach it** (the
cron line and the script), and **how do you drive it** (the fixture suite that
runs the real code, and the files on a live box that show it working).

`TEMPLATE.md` is the shape shipped to target repos and is not an entry.

| feature | entry | primary lane |
|---|---|---|

| Fan-in assertion for the merge conductor and reconcile-prs (BH-186) | [2026-09-24-BH-186-fanin.md](../notes/2026-09-24-BH-186-fanin.md) | No screen. `bin/lib/fanin.sh`, wired in `bin/merge-conductor.sh` `tick_project` (worker launch loop) and `bin/reconcile-prs.sh` (`rcr_one_pr`). Per-run ledger `state/fanin/<lane>/<scope>.<pid>`, deleted at run end. One `FANIN merge-conductor <slug>:` line per non-empty project tick in `logs/merge-conductor.log`, one `FANIN reconcile-prs fleet:` line per non-empty tick in `logs/reconcile.log`, and `ALERT FANIN …` through `bin/lane-alert.sh --severity warn` only on a mismatch. Live probe on the hub: `grep -c FANIN logs/merge-conductor.log logs/reconcile.log` > 0 after the first ticks, and `grep 'ALERT FANIN' logs/ALERTS.log` empty on a healthy fleet. Verified by `tests/fanin.bh-186-test.sh` (lib cases, and real conductor and reconcile ticks) |

| grok is updated daily on every box, floored on the hub, and its 1.0.x cost row prices (BH-1494: on 2026-09-22 six boxes ran grok 0.2.118, project-6 1.0.5, project-1-1 1.0.13 and small none at all, against a current 1.0.40 — the daily updater and the hub's floor check covered claude and codex only) | [../notes/2026-09-22-BH-1494-grok-cli-daily-update.md](../notes/2026-09-22-BH-1494-grok-cli-daily-update.md) | `bin/cli-update.sh` on every box (lane `update-runners`, `17 5 * * *`, via `bin/update-runners.sh`) updates `CLI_UPDATE_CLIS`, default `claude codex grok`; grok has no npm package, so it is re-installed with `curl -fsSL https://x.ai/cli/install.sh \| bash` (override `CLI_UPDATE_GROK_INSTALLER`, tests only), read through `~/.grok/bin/grok` BEFORE PATH because that is what the lanes run, and the previous release is kept one cycle at `~/.grok/downloads.prev` (rotated only when the version moved; the rollback `mv` is printed into `logs/cli-update.log`). Rows land in `logs/cli-versions.csv` and the `cli_versions` DB table; rc is non-zero only on WORSE or MISSING. The hub's `bin/cli-version-check.sh` (lane `cli-version-check`, `40 5 * * *`) probes the `clis` keys of `catalog/cli-min-versions.json` — claude, codex, grok — reading grok at `${CLI_CHECK_HOME:-$HOME}/.grok/bin/grok` first in both the cron and login views, and writes ONE `logs/ALERTS.log` line per box below a floor (`grok-4.6`/`grok-4.7` at 1.0.40, `gpt-6-astra` at 0.155.1), behind the hub, missing a CLI or split; `state/cli-versions-fleet.tsv` carries a row per box per CLI. Cost side: `bin/grok-usage.sh [--envelope <file>]` reads the CLI's `--output-format json` result document first and falls through to the ledger walk for every shape that is not unambiguously a usage envelope, and `bin/agent-call.sh`'s grok lane asks for that envelope only under `GROK_OUTPUT_JSON=1` (default `0`, because which field carries the answer text is unverified). Pinned by `tests/cli-update.bh-1494-test.sh`, `tests/cli-version-check.bh-1494-test.sh` and `tests/cost-grok-usage.bh-1494-test.sh` |

Exit 0 ok or already probed (job ledger kind `grok-envelope-probe`, key `<version>:<model>`), 1 refused or billed-fail, 3 no grok, below the floor or no `grok.default` route, 4 ledger unreadable or unwritable, 5 flip read-back mismatch; every non-zero exit is one `logs/ALERTS.log` line. Every billed or refused probe call writes one `logs/cost.csv` row, stage `grok-envelope-probe`, runner `grok`, note `probe:<verdict>:<detail>`, priced by `cost_row_price` (blank plus `UNPRICED:*` when it cannot be priced, `0` only for a refusal with no usage), before the ledger verdict; a run that stops before the call writes none. Capture goes to `state/grok-probe/<version>/{envelope.json,unified.jsonl,PROVENANCE.md}`. `--flip` writes settings row `lib.agent-call.grok_output_json` = `1` and reads it back (`GET /api/settings/lib.agent-call.grok_output_json`). grok rows: `GET /api/metrics/cli-versions?cli=grok` (history only, no `box_status` column). | Mergify queue-time measurement (BH-1673.3): how long the last N merged PRs of a repo waited between entering the merge queue and merging, read over GitHub REST only | [../MERGIFY.md](../MERGIFY.md) | `bin/mergify-queue-time.sh <owner/repo> [--last N]` (N defaults to 20; operator-invoked, no cron caller). One TSV row per merged PR, newest `merged_at` first: `pr queued_at merged_at minutes source`, where source is `queued` (the last Mergify `queued` label at or before merge), `merge-queue` (our label, the fallback), `none` (never queued) or `error` (its events read failed). The final line is `median_minutes=<x\|none> measured=<k> unqueued=<u> wanted=<N> incomplete=<0\|1>`: the median over measured rows only, `unqueued` counts `none` rows and never a failed read, and `incomplete=1` (fewer than N merged PRs on the one pulls page, a failed events read, or a full 100-event page) means do not trust the median as final. rc 75 with no output = the pulls read failed or any read was rate limited; rc 2 = usage. Verified by `tests/mergify-queue-time.bh-1700-test.sh` (stub `gh`, the real CLI) |

| Pre-PR gate, late pass: after the review fix rounds and the pre-PR rebase, the `# >>> prepr late` block in `bin/run-build.sh` (between `# <<< pre-pr rebase` and the tier classification, before the push and PR open, no cron) runs `prepr_gate_late` on the head the PR opens with. | queue-rows flip preflight, build gate (BH-2339 / BH-2313.1): `bin/lib/qrf/build.sh`, run by `bin/queue-rows-flip-preflight.sh build` before `build_queue_rows_only` goes to 1. It lists the live rows (`GET /api/queue?status=claimed&limit=500` and `?status=queued&limit=500`); a non-2xx is `[FAIL] build: live probe … http=<code>`, a list at 500 rows is `incomplete`, and either one skips parity. Then every `state/fly/<p>-active.json` `.key` needs a `claimed` row and every `state/build-queue/<p>/<KEY>.json` needs a `queued` or `claimed` row; a missing one is a `[FAIL]` naming the file and `queue_put`, and a row in another status is a `[FAIL]` naming that status. A live row no file on this box names is an info line, never a FAIL | none (no UI); operator-run on the hub and on each `fleet.conf` box that holds `state/fly/*-active.json` or `state/build-queue/` files; no cron line | probe: `HARNESS=/root/harness bin/queue-rows-flip-preflight.sh build` prints `[PASS] live probe /api/queue?status=claimed&limit=500 http=200 rows=N`, the same for `queued`, `[PASS] row parity: <n> file entries on this box, every one has a row` and `RESULT: READY build` (rc 0), or the `[FAIL] build:` lines and `RESULT: NOT READY build` (rc 1); GETs only, nothing written under `$HARNESS`; proven by `tests/queue-rows-flip-preflight-build.bh-2313-test.sh` |
| queue-rows flip preflight, merge gate (BH-2340 / BH-2313.2): `bin/lib/qrf/merge.sh`, the `merge_queue_rows_only` check run by `bin/queue-rows-flip-preflight.sh merge`. Per project directory under `state/merge-queue/` it lists the live statuses (BH-3221): GET `/api/merge-queue?project=<p>&status=queued&limit=500` and `…&status=blocked&limit=500`, array `.merge_queue`. It does not send the rows-only reader's unfiltered `mq_queue_list` → `merge_queue_list <p>` read, because the worker keeps landed/dropped rows and that list sits at its default limit of 200; the route cannot page, so a live status at 500 rows is incomplete. It checks the live rows are a superset of the files: a plain `<pr>` needs a `queued` row, a `<pr>.blocked` needs a `blocked` row (it shadows a plain `<pr>`), `*.absent` and non-numeric names are skipped; a file whose row is landed/dropped reads as having no row; a queued/blocked row with no file is info, never a FAIL; with no project directory it sends one GET `/api/merge-queue?status=queued` liveness probe | none (no UI); operator-run on each `fleet.conf` box that holds `state/merge-queue/`; no cron line | probe: `HARNESS=/root/harness bin/queue-rows-flip-preflight.sh merge`. READY prints `[PASS] live probe /api/merge-queue?project=<p>&status=<queued|blocked>&limit=500 http=200 rows=<n>` (one per status), `[PASS] row parity <p>: <n> file(s) matched` per project and `RESULT: READY merge`, exit 0. NOT READY prints `[FAIL] merge: state/merge-queue/<p>/<file> has no row — fix: merge_queue_put <p> <pr>` (a `.blocked` file names `merge_queue_set <p> <pr> blocked`, after `merge_queue_put` unless the row is `queued`), `[FAIL] merge: live probe … http=<code>` on a 503/1101/HTML answer to either read (that project's parity is then `not checked`, never matched), or `incomplete: rows=500 = limit 500`, then `RESULT: NOT READY merge`, exit 1. GETs only, nothing written; proven by `tests/queue-rows-flip-preflight-merge.bh-2313-test.sh` and `tests/qrf-merge-live.bh-3221-test.sh` |

A row with no file is info only | none (no UI); operator-run on the hub and on each `fleet.conf` box that holds `state/deploy-queue/`; no cron line | probe: `HARNESS=/root/harness bin/queue-rows-flip-preflight.sh deploy` prints `knob: deploy_queue_rows=<v>`, two `[PASS] live probe /api/deploy-queue?status=<queued/claimed>&limit=200 http=200 rows=<n>` lines, then `[PASS] row parity: <n> deploy file(s), each with a queued/claimed row` and `RESULT: READY deploy` (exit 0), or `[FAIL] deploy: row parity: state/deploy-queue/<p>/<name> has no queued/claimed deploy_queue row. | Mission Control main-red banner + audited CLEAR on /p/&lt;project&gt; (BH-2329.3) | [../HARNESS-COORDINATION.md](../HARNESS-COORDINATION.md) | Nav: `/p/<project>`. Under the header row, one `.banner[role=alert]` per live main-red freeze for that project (matched on the row's `project`, or a flag named `main-red-<project>`): `MAIN IS RED — <slug>`, `<workflow> failed (job: <job>) on <sha8>` (commit link only when the row has `slug` and `sha`), `since <age>`, `RUN` (only when `run_url` is set), and a `CLEAR` button. CLEAR opens `role=dialog[name="CLEAR MAIN-RED"]` with a reason textarea; CLEAR stays disabled with "A reason is required" until one is typed, and Escape cancels. Routes: `GET /api/coord/main-red` → worker `GET /api/main-red` (polled every 30 s), `POST /api/coord/main-red/clear` → worker `POST /api/main-red/clear` (proxy stamps `actor:"console"`; a worker 400/409 is shown verbatim, never as success), and `PUT /api/main-red` (the detector's upsert; 405 from the browser). Seed a live row, both with the `HARNESS_SECRET` bearer: `PUT <jobs worker>/api/flags` with `{name:"main-red-<project>", active:1}` (the banner lists only names whose `pause_flags` row is active), then `PUT <jobs worker>/api/main-red` with `{name:"main-red-<project>", active:1, project, slug, sha, workflow, job, run_url, since}` for the detail. If the worker cannot answer, the banner shows only a muted "main-red status unavailable". Detector log: `logs/main-red.log`; a publish failure reads `<name>: could not be published to the coordination DB` (`bin/main-red-detector.sh`). Worker routes verified by `worker/jobs/test/main-red.bh-2372.test.js` via `cd worker/jobs && node --test test/main-red.bh-2372.test.js`; console verified by `console-ui/tests/main-red-proxy.bh-2329.test.js` and `console-ui/tests/main-red-banner.bh-2329.test.js` via `cd console-ui && npm test` |
| Cost breaker card (BH-2331) | Nav COSTS → /costs → COST BREAKER (directly below SPEND) | Three reads through the coord proxy, each polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/settings` (the hub-published `cost.autohalt.effective` row, else the `lane.pipeline-health.cost_autohalt` row; BH-2397), `GET /api/coord/flags` (active `spend-hold-<project>` rows, and older `paused-<project>` rows, whose reason ends `(cost autohalt)` or `(zero-merge breaker)`; BH-5526), `GET /api/coord/call-guard?hours=24`. AUTOHALT line reads `ON`/`OFF`/`UNKNOWN` (UNKNOWN when the settings read failed) with its source, a "change in Settings" link to `/settings/pipeline-health`, and, only while the hub has not published `cost.autohalt.effective`, a note that the hub file `flags/cost-autohalt-off` is not visible here (BH-2397). TRIPS is a `SortableTable` (PROJECT `Link` to `/p/<project>`, KIND `HOLD` for a spend-hold row or `PAUSE` for an older paused row (BH-5526), WHY, SINCE, ACTOR; default SINCE descending); empty state "no project is paused by the breaker" with the note "Only spend-hold-<project> rows (and older paused-<project> rows) whose reason pipeline-health wrote are listed here."; a failed flags read is the ErrorBox, never the empty copy. CALL GUARD · 24H is a `SortableTable wide` (PROJECT link, STAGE, FINGERPRINT, PAID, REFUSED with `span.tag.tag--warn` "CAP HIT" when refusals since the last reset > 0 (BH-2397), LAST, RESET button); default REFUSED descending; a "first 200 keys" note at the worker's LIMIT; empty state "no guarded calls in the last 24h". RESET opens the in-app Modal (danger tone, focus in the REASON input, confirm disabled until the reason is non-blank, Escape cancels with no request) and sends `DELETE /api/coord/call-guard?project=&stage=&fp=&reason=`; the proxy stamps `actor=console` and forwards to the worker's audited reset; success reads "reset N paid row(s) for <project>/<stage>" and reloads the table, failure reads "RESET failed — <error>" |

| routing compare-and-swap on the worker (BH-2459): `PUT /api/routing` takes an optional `expected_updated_at`, the row's `updated_at` as the caller read it, compared exactly; when it no longer matches (or the row is gone) the worker answers 409 `{error: "routing changed underneath you", routing: <the row as it is now, or null>}` and writes neither the row nor a `routing_changes` row, including when the change lands between the worker's own read and its write; a guarded PUT never creates a row; an empty, non-string or over-64-char value is a 400; absent or `null`, the PUT is the same upsert as before. `updated_at` is stamped to the second, so a change in the same second as the write the caller read is not detected. The console's HALT write sends it (BH-2488) | none (no UI); worker `worker/jobs/src/routes/routing.js` | probe: `curl -s -X PUT -H "Authorization: Bearer $HARNESS_SECRET" -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"project":"<p>","builder":"<current>","pool_app":<current>,"halted":<current>,"expected_updated_at":"1970-01-01T00:00:00Z"}' "$JOBS_URL/api/routing"` answers 409 with the current row, and the row is unchanged; proven by `worker/jobs/test/routing-cas.bh-2459.test.js` |

| Cost caps table (BH-2511, BH-2566) | Nav COSTS → /costs → CAPS (directly below COST BREAKER) | Read `GET /api/coord/settings`, polled every 60s; every `conf.cost-caps.*` and `conf.lane-caps.*` row is one table row. CAPS is a `SortableTable wide` (scrolls inside its own container at 375px): SCOPE (project/lane/fleet, plus ` · box <b>` for a box-suffixed key), SUBJECT (project → `Link` to `/p/<project>`, lane → `Link` to `/settings/<lane>`, fleet keys plain text), KIND (`cost-caps.budget`, `lane-caps.noise`, …), CATALOG (the `registry-seed` row's value, else `—`), OVERRIDE (an operator row's value, else `—`), APPLIES (override, else catalog), BY, UPDATED (relative, ISO on hover), EDIT; default SCOPE ascending. EDIT is offered on fleet rows only; box-suffixed and unknown-shaped keys read "display only". EDIT opens the in-app Modal "EDIT CAP": the key, a VALUE input (focused on open, prefilled with APPLIES) then a REASON input, the kind's grammar and a hint; SAVE stays disabled until the reason is non-blank and the value parses (same grammar as `bin/lib/cost-budget.sh` / `bin/lib/lane-caps.sh`); Escape cancels with no request. SAVE sends `PUT /api/coord/cost-caps {key,value,reason}`; the proxy stamps `actor=console`. Success reads "cap <key> set to <value>" and reloads the table; failure reads "EDIT failed — <worker error>" and does not reload ("EDIT failed — not implemented" while the worker route is the BH-2563 stub). States: Skeleton while loading; a failed settings read is the ErrorBox, never the empty copy; no cap rows is "no cap rows yet — run bin/cost-caps-seed.sh on the hub". Footnote always: "a box-local flags/cost-caps.conf or flags/lane-caps.conf is not visible here; an override row beats it" |
| Costs cap history (BH-2511 / BH-2567) | Nav FLEET → COSTS → /costs, scroll past COST BREAKER and CAPS to the CAP HISTORY section | One read, `GET /api/coord/cost-cap-changes?limit=200` through the coord proxy, on mount and polled every 30s (`lib/poll.js`, backs off on failure, pauses while hidden), so a CAPS edit shows up without the two sections sharing state. The table is a `SortableTable wide`, default WHEN descending, every header a sort button with aria-sort: WHEN (date + `HH:MMZ`, title the raw `at`, sorted on the raw ISO string), KEY (a button: click re-reads `?key=<key>&limit=200` and shows only that key's rows), BEFORE (`—` for a created cap), AFTER, ACTOR, REASON (`—` when none). An active filter shows a `key: <key>` chip and a "clear filter" button that re-reads without `key=`. States: loading skeleton (also while the answer for a new filter is in flight); failed read is the ErrorBox with RETRY, never the empty copy, and a failed refresh keeps the last rows under it; a body without a `changes` array is the ErrorBox "unexpected response from /api/coord/cost-cap-changes"; zero rows reads "no cap changes yet" ("no cap changes for <key> yet" when filtered); "showing the newest N" only when the body says `truncated: true` |
| Models page control: remove a hub route, override a model's price source, and list the stages with no `stage.*` route (BH-2512) | [../MODEL-ROUTES.md](../MODEL-ROUTES.md) | console MODELS (`/models`, nav MODELS) → UNROUTED STAGES section after STAGE RUNNERS: one row per stage the cost ledger saw in the last 7 days with no rung-0 `stage.<x>` row at its project or the hub, tagged DEFAULTS TO CLAUDE, STAGE links to `/models/stage.<x>` (SAVE LADDER there routes it), RAN ON is the runners the ledger recorded; empty state "EVERY STAGE IS ROUTED", a failed ledger read shows "could not read the cost ledger", over 500 pairs says "showing the first 500"; `/models?project=<p>` scopes it to that project. Hub tab only: MODEL PRICES section, per model MARK VENDOR-DIRECT / USE OPENROUTER (in-app confirm modal) → `PUT /api/coord/model-prices/source`. `/models/<role>` on the hub scope when the hub holds rows (a ladder or gapped rows): REMOVE ROUTE (confirm modal lists the rungs) → `POST /api/coord/model-routes/remove`; never on `?project=`. Worker: `GET /api/model-routes?unrouted_since=YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` adds `unrouted_stages`, `unrouted_since`, `unrouted_truncated` (or `unrouted_stages: null` + `unrouted_error`), 400 on any other shape, no key without the param. Proxy: `console-ui/functions/api/coord/[[path]].js` WRITES rows for both writes; verified by `bash tests/models-control.bh-2512-test.sh` (real proxy → real worker), `worker/jobs/test/model-routes-unrouted.bh-2512.test.js` via `cd worker/jobs && npm test`, and `console-ui/tests/models-control.bh-2512.test.js` |
| Mission Control CONTROLS (BH-2513.4 / BH-2621: one generic editor per config kind in `CONTROL_KINDS` — review-gate thresholds, concurrency rules, retry policies, phase contracts) | [mission-control-settings.md](mission-control-settings.md) | Nav SETTINGS → the CONTROLS link row under the page title (`a.tab[href^="/controls/"]`, one per kind: `/controls/review-gate`, `/controls/rules`, `/controls/retry-policies`, `/controls/contracts`) → `/controls/<kind>` (`console-ui/src/screens/Controls.js`, route `/controls/:kind` in `console-ui/src/app.js`; the SETTINGS tab stays lit). Reads `GET /api/coord/<kind path>` (the proxy forwards to the worker's `/api/review-gate`, `/api/rules`, `/api/retry-policies`, `/api/contracts`) and writes one row with `PUT /api/coord/<put path>` (`review-gate/thresholds` for the gate, the list path for the rest); the coord proxy stamps the actor. Until BH-2619 adds those four PUTs to the proxy's WRITES table, every SAVE is refused by the proxy with 405 "method PUT not allowed on /api/coord/…" and that shows inside the modal. Selectors: the kind tabs `nav.tabs a.tab` (`a.tab--on` + `aria-current="page"` on the active one), one `SortableTable wide` with a `th[aria-sort] button` per key and edit field (default sort on the first key field), the identity cell's `button.btn.btn--sm` "EDIT <row name>", the `NEW` button, and in the in-app Modal the key inputs (`readonly` on EDIT, editable or a `select` over the response's `keySelect` list on NEW — `scopes` for rules), the `textarea[name=reason]` REASON and a SAVE confirm disabled while `controlBody` answers an error or the PUT is pending. States: skeleton on first load; a failed read is the "could not load" banner with RETRY (never the empty copy); a response without the kind's list key is "unexpected response from <path>" with RETRY; zero rows is "no <title> rows on record yet" with NEW kept; a refusal (worker 400 `{error}`, proxy 405, network) shows "not saved — <error>" inside the modal with the draft kept, and only a 2xx closes it and reloads the list; Escape cancels. `/controls/<unknown>` is NO SUCH SCREEN. Suites: `console-ui/tests/controls-render.bh-2621.test.js`, `tests/controls-screen.bh-2513-test.sh` (no cron caller — an operator drives it) |
| Command history (BH-2514) | Nav NOW → /now → HISTORY → COMMANDS tab → /history/commands (also reachable from any `retry_command` row's TARGET, `/history/commands?id=<n>`) | One read, `GET /api/dash/commands?limit=200` through the verbatim Pages proxy, polled every 30s. Crumb `← NOW`, title HISTORY, tab strip RUNS (`/history`) · BUILDS (`/history?tab=builds`) · COMMANDS (active). The table is a `SortableTable wide`, default WHEN descending, every header a sort button with aria-sort: # (cell `span#cmd-<id>`), WHEN (date + `HH:MMZ`, title the raw timestamp), TYPE, PROJECT (`Link` to `/p/<project>` plus a `=` button that sets `?project=`), ACTOR (`payload.actor`/row actor, `—` when absent, never "console" unless the row says so, `=` button sets `?actor=`), STATUS (`tag--dead` for dead_letter/failed/refused, `tag--green` for done), OUTCOME (refusal, else result, truncated at 120 with full title), TARGET (job → `/jobs/<id>`, merge_pr/lead_review → `/pr/<project>/<n>`, proposal types → `/ideas/<project>/<slug>`, board types → `/p/<project>`, fleet-scope pause → `/jobs`, retry_command → `/history/commands?id=<cmd_id>`, chat → `/chat`, anything else `—`). Filters ride the URL: TYPE and STATUS chip rows (ANY clears), active filters as removable `key: value ×` chips; `?id=<n>` outlines that row (`tr.row--hl`) and scrolls to it, or shows "command #n is older than the loaded window". States: loading skeleton; failed read is the ErrorBox with RETRY, never the empty copy; a body without a `commands` array is the ErrorBox "unexpected response from /api/dash/commands"; zero rows reads "no console or chat command on record."; zero after filtering reads "no command matches these filters in the loaded rows."; footnote "latest N commands the console returned · sorting and filters apply to these rows only", plus "· older commands are not shown" at 200 rows |
| Identity pill + actor stamping (BH-2515) | Any route: the top bar, `.topbar__right`, first pill (left of WATCHDOG) | One read on load, `GET /api/coord/whoami` → `{actor, access, email}`, answered by the coord Pages function itself (never forwarded to harness-jobs; still behind the `dash` session check, 403 without it). Pill copy per `access`: `on` → the email (`span.pill`); `off` (Pages env `CF_ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN` or `CF_ACCESS_AUD` unset) → `ACCESS OFF · writes as console`; `unverified` (env set, no verifiable `Cf-Access-Jwt-Assertion` matching `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email`) → `IDENTITY UNVERIFIED · writes as console`; `unrecordable` (email outside `^[a-z0-9._@-]{1,96}$`) → `<email> · recorded as console`; all four warn states are `span.pill.pill--warn`; a failed read → `identity unknown`; before the read lands → `identity…` (`pill--muted`). The pill ellipsises at `max-width:40vw` so a long email cannot push the 375px bar into horizontal scroll; its `title` explains the state. Every admitted coord PUT/POST body and the call-guard DELETE query carry `actor` = that resolution (never the browser's value), and `POST /api/dash/action` bodies are stamped the same way; the box writes that actor on the `pause_board` / `pause_set` pauses row (`console` for anything outside the worker's actor charset). Proof: `console-ui/tests/access-identity.bh-2515.test.js`, `tests/access-identity-console.bh-2515-test.sh`. The live Access path needs Access in front of the host and both env vars set |

| Box and Fly machine ops from the console, plus the BOX COMMANDS history | [fly-machines.md](fly-machines.md) | Nav FLEET → MACHINES (`/machines`; `/machines?box=<name>` filters the history to one box). Buttons: ORGO BOXES ACTIONS column (BACKUP NOW, SYNC, CLI UPDATE) and each FLY · &lt;project&gt; table's ACTIONS column (STOP on started machines only, DESTROY on every row, disabled with title `machine's app unknown` when the app cannot be resolved). Every button opens the in-app confirm with a required REASON; CONFIRM stays disabled until it is non-blank, Escape cancels. Routes `POST /api/coord/box-commands` (the request) and `GET /api/coord/box-commands[?box=]` (history, polled every 30 s); source `console-ui/src/lib/box-commands.js`, `console-ui/src/screens/Machines.js`. Executor: the per-box lane `bin/box-ops.sh` is sibling BH-2660 and is not part of this slice; its knobs `BOX_OPS_TIMEOUT_S` and `BOX_OPS_MAX_PER_TICK` are that sibling's, and the claim TTL is `settings.box_commands_claim_ttl_s` (worker sibling BH-2659, 1800 s fallback). Verify: pick an ORGO box → BACKUP NOW → type a reason → CONFIRM → banner `queued for <box>` (or `already queued (#id)` on a repeat, or `BACKUP NOW failed: http <n>` while the worker route is undeployed) → the row shows in BOX COMMANDS with STATUS REQUESTED. BH-5750 box controls (owner only: the worker answers 403 `owner_only` unless the Access-verified actor is in the worker secret `BOX_OPS_OWNERS`, and the warn banner shows it). ORGO BOXES: every row with an Orgo box id gets RESTART, RESIZE, STOP and RETIRE beside the three above; the id is the row name without the prefix every non-hub row shares and without a trailing `-N` (`<prefix>-project-3-2` → `project-3`), and the `hub` row and an all-number rest (the hub's own fleet name) get no Orgo buttons. All four are queued for box `hub` with the Orgo id as target. RESTART and STOP open the reason confirm; STOP says it never lifts the pause. RESIZE opens a form (ACCOUNT, a select over the Orgo read's accounts, preset to the computer's own; VCPUS, MEMORY GB, DISK GB, blank keeps a size; REASON) → GET PRICE posts `orgo_resize_quote` → the `orgo-quote` dialog says `pricing… queued for hub (quote #id)` and polls `GET /api/coord/box-commands?box=hub` every 5 s for 180 s at most (stopped when the dialog closes) → it shows the quote's `PLAN cost +$N/mo …` / `COST: resize …` or `NEEDS PURCHASE: …` field verbatim; CONFIRM (RESIZE) is enabled only on an ok quote and posts `orgo_resize` with `quote_id` = the quote row id and `confirm_monthly_usd` = the figure from that line (the worker's rule; an ok quote with no cost line is `0`); a failed quote or 180 s with no answer (`no price yet`) leaves it disabled and posts nothing more; a worker 409 `stale_quote` / `open_command_differs` shows in the banner. RETIRE (danger) has REASON, MOVE TO, IP and a delete checkbox; delete needs the box id typed into `TYPE <id> TO DELETE` and focus stays in that field. New columns ORGO STATE (`running`, `stopped`, … from `GET /api/coord/orgo-computers` matched on computer name = row name; `unknown` with no record, `gone <age>` for a `gone_at` row, `<state> · stale <age>` when `seen_at` is over 15 min old, `not available` when the route answers 404/503, `read failed` on any other error) and SPECS (`4c/16gb/100gb`), both sortable. LAUNCH BOX section (`data-testid=launch-box`; BOX ID, PROJECT, ACCOUNT, VCPUS, MEMORY GB, DISK GB, REASON): GET PRICE stays disabled until every field is set (0 or 2.5 is refused) → posts `orgo_launch_quote` → the dialog shows the `capacity: …` and `box-launch: … usd_mo=N …` fields verbatim → LAUNCH enabled only on an ok quote with `usd_mo`, posts `orgo_launch` with the token. Each FLY · &lt;project&gt; section ends in `data-testid=fly-pool-controls`: a project on the pool (a `pool-<p>` or `<p>-pool` app, or a `pool` machine; the payload has no pool flag) gets POOL CAP + SET (`fly_pool_cap`, a whole number above 0), one off it gets ONBOARD TO POOL (`fly_onboard`); both confirm with `Fly bills per running machine; no fixed monthly price.` and no quote. BOX COMMANDS: a DETAIL `NEXT:` field renders on its own line in a `<code>` block; a `fail` row whose detail has `WAITING:`, `NEEDS PURCHASE:` or `refused:` shows WAITING, NEEDS PURCHASE or REFUSED as its STATUS tag. Executor: `bin/box-ops.sh` on the hub (BH-5750 C2/C5/C6); the Orgo read is `bin/orgo-state.sh` (C4). Source `console-ui/src/ui/BoxOpForms.js`, `console-ui/src/lib/box-op-detail.js`, `console-ui/src/lib/orgo-state.js`. Tests `console-ui/tests/box-commands.bh-2518.test.js`, `tests/box-commands-console.bh-2518-test.sh`, `console-ui/tests/machines-orgo-actions.bh-5750.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/machines-orgo-state.bh-5750.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/machines-launch.bh-5750.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/machines-fly-pool.bh-5750.test.js`, `tests/machines-orgo-ui.bh-5750-test.sh` |

A SortableTable (inside `.tablewrap`) with PROJECT (links to `/p/<project>`), THREAD, ACTOR, UPDATED and an unsortable edit column; REFRESH in the section head reloads once (no polling). EDIT turns the row's thread into `input[aria-label^="new thread id"]` with SAVE / CANCEL; an id that is not a positive integer, or the current id, is refused inline and sends nothing; a valid id opens `role=dialog[aria-label="CHAT BINDINGS"]` reading "Move <project> from thread A to B?" with a required REASON input and CANCEL / MOVE; MOVE is disabled until the reason is filled (BH-2995), then sends one PUT `{project, thread_id, reason}` and reloads. Routes: `GET`/`PUT /api/coord/telegram-topics` → worker `GET`/`PUT /api/telegram-topics` (`worker/jobs/src/routes/telegram-topics.js`, GET answers `{topics, rows, as_of}`), table `telegram_topics`. Precondition: at least one `telegram_topics` row; with none the section reads "No chat bindings yet", and a failed load shows "could not load" with RETRY. A failed push is one log line (`ci-health push: push failed …` in `logs/ci-minutes-watch.log`, `ci-health: runners snapshot push failed …` in `logs/testhost-tick.log`) and never changes the lane's rc or its page/alert. Verified by `worker/jobs/test/routes/ci-health.bh-2633.test.js`, `tests/ci-health-route.bh-2633-test.sh` and `tests/ci-health-push.bh-2633-test.sh`; probe: `curl -s -H "authorization: Bearer $HARNESS_SECRET" "$JOBS_URL/api/ci-health?repo=example-org/cromford" \| jq '.rows[].metric'` lists `runners` after the next `*/5` testhost tick |
| CI health panel (BH-2634) | Nav CI → /ci; a repo → /ci?repo=<slug> (slug URL-encoded, e.g. `/ci?repo=example-org%2Fcromford`) | Three reads through the coord proxy, each polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/ci-health`, `GET /api/coord/metrics/flakes?since=<today-7d>&limit=2000`, `GET /api/coord/metrics/flakes/quarantine?active=1`. CI HEALTH is a `SortableTable wide` (REPO `Link` to `/ci?repo=<slug>`, MINUTES 24H, RUNNERS online/total, FLAKES 7D and FLAKE RATE in distinct heads, QUARANTINED); a missing value is `span.na` "—" titled `unread` or `not pushed`, never 0. Skeleton until every read answers; all three failed is the ErrorBox (worker offline, or the ci-health/quarantine routes not deployed yet); some failed is a footnote per failed read ("minutes and runners unreadable: …", "quarantine unreadable: …") over the table; a "first 2000 flake rows only" note at the worker's limit; zero rows is the Empty "no CI health pushed yet". With `?repo=` a FLAKES · <repo> section adds `GET /api/coord/metrics/flakes?repo=&since=&limit=2000` and `GET …/quarantine?repo=&active=1`: a `SortableTable` (TEST button with `aria-expanded`, FLAKY HEADS, LAST SEEN, QUARANTINE ON/OFF tag plus an ON/OFF button, disabled with a title for `job:`/`workflow:` names and when the quarantine read failed); empty "no flaky tests for <repo> in the last 7 days"; a failed flakes read is the ErrorBox. Clicking a TEST opens HISTORY · <test> from `GET /api/coord/metrics/flakes/quarantine/changes?repo=&test=&limit=50` (WHEN, CHANGE `off → on` / `— → on`, ACTOR, REASON; newest first; empty "no quarantine changes yet"). The toggle opens the in-app Modal (focus in the REASON input, confirm disabled until the reason is non-blank, Escape cancels with no request) and sends `PUT /api/coord/metrics/flakes/quarantine` with `{repo, test, active, reason}`; the proxy stamps `actor`. Success needs the worker's `ok: true`, shows a `role="status"` banner and reloads; anything else shows "quarantine failed — <error>" and does not reload. MERGE-QUEUE BATCHES (BH-4972), always below CI HEALTH, adds a fourth read polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/ci-batches?[repo=<slug>&]since=<today-30d>&limit=5000` (BH-4971's ledger; `?repo=` scopes it, none reads every repo). Skeleton until it answers; a failed read or a payload with no `rows` array is the ErrorBox with RETRY (an unapplied table shows "ci_batches schema not applied" there, and the rest of /ci still renders); zero rows is the Empty "no merge-queue batches recorded yet" with no median line. With rows: the line "7-day median batch minutes: before X (n) · after Y (n)" (billed minutes of every batch in the 7 days either side of the first `batch-selected` batch; a side with no measured batch reads "not enough batches yet", or "before the loaded window" when the 30-day read or a truncated read cut it), a "first 5000 batches only" footnote when the read is truncated, then a `SortableTable wide` newest first: RUN (`#<run_id>` link to `https://github.com/<repo>/actions/runs/<run_id>`, new tab; plain text titled "repo unreadable" when the repo or id is not readable), WHEN, MODE, SELECTED / TOTAL (`12 / 340`, "—" for a missing count), ESCALATION (reason text, "—" for none), DURATION, BILLED min, CONCLUSION; every header sorts on the shown value with aria-sort, missing values sink. |

Routes: `POST /api/coord/settings/seed-registry/apply` `{plan_fp, reason}` and `PUT /api/coord/telegram-topics` `{project, thread_id, reason}`; the coord proxy answers `400 {"error":"reason required"}` to a blank or missing reason and forwards nothing. Precondition: a plan with pending rows (SEED REGISTRY) and at least one `telegram_topics` row (CHAT BINDINGS). Sources `console-ui/functions/api/coord/[[path]].js`, `console-ui/src/lib/registry-seed.js`, `console-ui/src/lib/chat-bindings.js`, `console-ui/src/screens/hub/SeedRegistry.js`, `console-ui/src/screens/hub/ChatBindings.js`; driven by `console-ui/tests/hub-write-reason-proxy.bh-2995.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/hub-reason-panels.bh-2995.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/seed-registry-panel.bh-2521.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/chat-bindings-panel.bh-2521.test.js` |

| Merge-queue row edit and held-page release through the coord proxy (BH-3157, BH-2325.2) | [2026-09-28-BH-3157-proxy-writes.md](../notes/2026-09-28-BH-3157-proxy-writes.md) | No screen yet: the merge-queue action cells (`MqRowActions`, `HeldReleaseButton` in `console-ui/src/screens/merge-queue-actions.js`) are still null stubs from BH-3155. Browser path: `PATCH /api/coord/merge-queue` `{project, pr, status, reason}` (status one of queued, blocked, dropped; `landed` is a 400) and `POST /api/coord/conductor/held/release` `{repo, number, state, head, kind, why}`. The proxy forwards exactly those fields plus its own `actor` (`console`, or the Access email) to `PATCH /api/merge-queue` and `POST /api/conductor/held/release` on the jobs worker, and returns the worker's status as is. A bad type or a blank reason/why is a 400 that forwards nothing. `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE merge-queue` and every write to `conductor/held` stay 405. Verified by `console-ui/tests/merge-queue-proxy.bh-3157.test.js` (`cd console-ui && node --test tests/merge-queue-proxy.bh-3157.test.js`) |
| Merge queue screen (BH-3158) | Nav KANBAN → the MERGE QUEUE button in the Kanban header → /merge-queue (the KANBAN tab stays lit); a PROJECT cell → /merge-queue?project=<p> (URL-encoded), where an ALL PROJECTS crumb resets it; ← KANBAN crumb back | Read-only. Eight reads through the coord proxy, each polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/merge-queue?project=&status=<queued\|blocked\|dropped>&limit=500` (three reads; landed is never read), `GET /api/coord/conductor/held?limit=500`, `GET /api/coord/config-changes?kind=merge_queue&limit=50` and `?kind=held_page&limit=50`, `GET /api/coord/settings/merge_queue_rows_only`, `GET /api/coord/risk-tiers?limit=200` (BH-5022). A `.banner[role=status]` states the rows-only mode: "Rows-only is ON …", "Rows-only is OFF …", "No merge_queue_rows_only row …" (no settings row, each box uses its own env) or "Rows-only setting unreadable: <err> …". MERGE QUEUE is a `SortableTable wide`, oldest `enqueued_at` first (PROJECT `Link` to the filtered screen, PR `Link` to `/pr/<p>/<n>`, STATUS `span.tag` with `tag--warn` for blocked, REASON, ENQUEUED BY, AGE, UPDATED, ACTIONS = `MqRowActions`, empty until the actions sibling fills the stub). A failed status read is a footnote "<status> rows unreadable: <err>"; all three failed is the ErrorBox; a status at 500 rows notes "first 500 <status> rows only: the newest ones are missing"; zero rows is the Empty "nothing queued, blocked or dropped", or "no rows from the readable statuses" when a read failed. RISK TIERS (BH-5022, between MERGE QUEUE and HELD PAGES): ALL / LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH buttons (`button.tab` with `aria-pressed`, wrapping) drive `?tier=low\|medium\|high` (kept beside `?project=`; any other value is ALL) and filter the one read client-side; a `SortableTable wide`, newest WHEN first (PR `Link` to `/pr/<project>/<n>`, project = the row's project or the repo's name part; HEAD sha12 with the full sha in title; TIER uppercase tag sorting low < medium < high; REASONS; ACTION; MODE; WHEN); `truncated` notes "newest 200 classified heads only"; empty "no PR heads classified yet" or "no <tier>-risk heads"; a failed read is the ErrorBox with RETRY; fleet-wide under `?project=`. HELD PAGES is a `SortableTable wide` (PR as an external `github.com/<repo>/pull/<n>` link, STATE, KIND, MODE, HELD SINCE, LAST SEEN, TEXT, a `HeldReleaseButton` cell), with "<shown> of <n_total>" from the worker ("<n> shown, total unknown" without one); empty "no held pages"; a failed read is the ErrorBox. RECENT CONTROL CHANGES is a `SortableTable wide`, newest first (WHEN, WHAT `merge_queue <name>` / `held_page <name>`, CHANGE `queued → blocked` / `released`, ACTOR, REASON); one kind failed is a footnote, both is the ErrorBox; empty "no merge-queue or held-page changes yet". Held pages, changes and risk tiers stay fleet-wide under `?project=`. Skeleton until each section's reads answer |
| Merge-queue row actions + held-page RELEASE (BH-3159, BH-2325.4) | Nav → /merge-queue (the screen sibling #3195 adds; until it merges, its preview) → ACTIONS cell on a merge-queue row (HOLD/DROP on `queued`, RESUME/DROP on `blocked`, RESUME on `dropped`, nothing on `landed`) and RELEASE on a held-pages row (only when repo, number, state, head and kind are all present). Source: `console-ui/src/screens/merge-queue-actions.js` (`MqRowActions`, `HeldReleaseButton`, `MqActionForm`) over `console-ui/src/lib/merge-queue-actions.js` | Every button opens the in-app Modal (`.modal`, never a native dialog): the title is `<ACTION> PR` or `RELEASE HELD PAGE`, the body names `project#pr` (held: `repo#number (state, kind)`) and the effect sentence for the current rows-only mode (`on`/`off`/`unread`, from `bin/merge-queue.sh` and `bin/lib/mc/log.sh`; see [2026-09-28-BH-2325-merge-queue-actions.md](../notes/2026-09-28-BH-2325-merge-queue-actions.md)). Focus starts in the REASON input (`.modal__body input`) and stays there through typing and a parent rerender; CONFIRM (the last `.modal__actions` button) is disabled until the reason is non-blank; CANCEL, Escape and a scrim click send nothing. CONFIRM sends `PATCH /api/coord/merge-queue` `{project, pr, status, reason}` or `POST /api/coord/conductor/held/release` `{repo, number, state, head, kind, why}` ([conductor-held-pages-api.md](conductor-held-pages-api.md)). Success is only `merge.status` equal to the target (inline "`project#pr` is now `<status>`") or a numeric `released` (`0` reads "nothing was held under that key (already released)"); either reloads the list through `onDone`. A thrown or non-2xx answer reads "`<ACTION>` failed — `<error>`", a 2xx without the field reads "`<ACTION>` was not confirmed by the worker", and neither reloads. PROD QA: drive up to CANCEL only; never HOLD, DROP, RESUME or RELEASE a real row. Drive: `cd console-ui && node --test tests/merge-queue-actions.bh-2325.test.js`; shell: `tests/merge-queue-actions.bh-2325-test.sh` |

| Factory stop, slice 2 (BH-3710 of BH-3687): the merge and deploy lanes are tagged in their own `catalog/lanes.d/` manifests. `"factory_stop": "merge"`: merge-conductor (which also covers the tier-2 Mergify auto-enqueue it runs), merge-conductor-backstop, merge-queue, batch-resolve, rebase-train, repair-on-push (BH-6963: it pushes rebases to PR branches, as rebase-train does), merge-conductor-wake (BH-6975: it starts keyed conductor ticks, as merge-conductor-backstop does). `"factory_stop": "deploy"`: deploy-train, fleet-propagate, apply-migrations, migration-autopilot. With `switch.factory-stop` ON each logs `[lane-gate] <id>[/<project>] SKIPPED — factory-stop (merge lane\|deploy lane; settings switch.factory-stop)` in `logs/lane-gate.log` and exits before its work. fleet-propagate is gated only in `MODE=deploy` and apply-migrations only in `MODE=cron`; a manual run in another mode is not stopped. PRs already inside Mergify's own queue may still be landed by Mergify, which is outside the harness. | A proven human verdict and the two human-verdict switches are tamper-evident against a direct D1 write (BH-3846). `POST /api/verdicts` signs a `human-token` row into `verdicts.human_sig` (HMAC under the worker secret `HUMAN_VERDICT_SIG_KEY`) and answers `human_signed`; with the key unset it writes unsigned, or refuses 503 under `human_verdict_sig_mode=enforce`. `GET /api/verdicts` adds `human_proven` to `rows[]` and `standing`, and a row repeating a signature an earlier row in the PR carries (a whole-row replay) reads unproven; under `human_verdict_sig_mode=enforce` (seeds `shadow`) an unproven human MERGE/MERGE-WITH-NOTES `standing` reads `source: lead`, `source_recorded: human`. `PUT /api/settings` of `human_verdict_mode` or `human_verdict_sig_mode` needs the human token, writes a `<key>.sig` row and answers `signed`; a PUT of a `.sig` key is 400, and `GET /api/settings` answers it `value: null, redacted: true`. A `.sig` that does not verify reads `enforce`, and the worker secret `HUMAN_VERDICT_ENFORCE=1` forces `enforce`; that floor is a required rollout step, since a D1 writer can delete or replay a `.sig`. No UI | [MERGE-CONDUCTOR.md](../MERGE-CONDUCTOR.md) | `sigKeyOf`, `humanProven`, `firstRowBySig`, `protectedMode`, `PROTECTED_HUMAN_KEYS` and the POST/GET handlers in `worker/jobs/src/routes/verdicts.js`; the protected-key guard, `.sig` upsert and `redactSig` in `worker/jobs/src/routes/settings.js`; `ADDED_COLUMNS.verdicts` and `CREATED_TABLES.verdicts` in `worker/jobs/src/lib/core.js`; the column and seed in `worker/jobs/schema.sql`; driven by `worker/jobs/test/verdicts-human-sig.bh-3846.test.js` |

One row per fleet.conf project for the hour the run started: the `builds` section from `bin/lib/utilization/section-builds.sh` (`running` from `GET /api/jobs?project=&status=running&kind=build&limit=500`, null when that read failed or hit the 500 cap; `pool_cap` from `bin/concurrency-plan.sh <p> --json` `.effective`; `free_slots` `"unlimited"`, `max(cap-running,0)` or null), a section that fails or prints a non-object as `basis.<name>:"unmeasured"`, verdict from `util_verdict` in `bin/lib/utilization.sh` (the full precedence since BH-4009, see the BH-3988.2 entry). Projects past the deadline are `deferred`, never written as zeros. Summary line `projects=N written=N failed=N deferred=N` in `logs/utilization-push.log`; a failed PUT is `failed` plus `put failed project=<p>` and never changes the lane's rc 0. Off: `flags/lane-off-utilization-push`, the `utilization-push-off` switch, `UTIL_PUSH_ENABLED=0` | `worker/jobs/src/routes/utilization.js` (`PUT /api/utilization` `{project, hour: YYYY-MM-DDTHH:00:00Z, verdict: starved\|paused\|blocked\|saturated\|busy\|unknown, payload: object ≤ 8192 bytes serialised, box?, retention_days?}` upserts on `(project, hour)` with `as_of` = request time and prunes that project's rows older than `retention_days` (default 35, clamped 7..90, a non-number is 400) in the same batch, answering `{ok, row, pruned}`, else 400; `GET /api/utilization` answers the newest row per project `{rows:[{project,hour,verdict,payload (parsed),box,as_of}], as_of}`, a stored payload that does not parse as `payload:null, payload_error:"unparseable"`; `?project=&since=` is the history form, see the BH-3988.5 row); table `utilization_snapshots` + `idx_util_hour` in `worker/jobs/schema.sql`, applied by `bin/harness-sync.sh` before `wrangler deploy`, else both handlers answer 503 `utilization_snapshots schema not applied`. `utilization_rows <project> <since>` is rc 0 with rows, 2 on `rows:[]`, 1 on anything else including a 404. | Utilization ticket buckets and verdict (BH-4009 / BH-3988.1: the `tickets` section and the full `util_verdict` precedence) | none (no UI; read the rows with `GET /api/utilization`). `bin/lib/utilization/section-tickets.sh` is loaded by the collector's `section-*.sh` loop in `bin/utilization-push.sh`, with no edit to the lane. `util_section_tickets <p>` prints `{tickets:{ready,blocked,held,parked,held_complete}, basis:{<bucket>:"unmeasured" for every null bucket}}`. On the GitHub-Issues lane (`fleet-conf.sh <p> board_prefix` is `-`) it reads open `ai-build` issues (PR items dropped, ≤3 pages of 100; a full third page is capped and null). `blocked` is a body line starting `Blocked-by` that names a bare `#N` which is in the list or which `gh_rest_issue_state` does not say is closed. `held` comes from `hold_classify` (`bin/lib/hold.sh`) over each candidate's comment thread, read with `gh_rest`. A candidate is not blocked, has no open PR whose head carries `<letters>-N`, and has no running build of N. `ready` is the rest, and `parked` is null. On the in-app lane (board_prefix a path) it makes two reads, `<prod><prefix>/boards` and `…/boards/<id>/tickets`, with the token from `bin/internal-token-for.sh`. Open means status not one of done closed cancelled canceled archived rejected shipped review. `blocked` is `blocked:true`. `parked` is `waitingOn` client or a `triageVerdict` other than ready/empty. `held` is null. Any failed read is null, never 0: rate limited (gh_rest 75), the board read, the PR list, a running-jobs read at its 500 cap (`ready` only), an unreadable hold thread (`held` and `ready`). Every GitHub call is a `gh api -i -X GET` through `gh_rest` with `GH_REST_OPTIONAL=1`. Knob `UTIL_HOLD_READS_MAX` (default 30, settings row `lib.utilization-tickets[.<p>].util_hold_reads_max`, `catalog/lanes.d/_lib.utilization-tickets.json`) caps the blocker-state plus hold-thread reads per project per run; past it the unchecked candidates count ready with `held_complete:false` | `util_verdict` in `bin/lib/utilization.sh`, first match wins: 1 `unknown` (`tickets.ready` or `builds.free_slots` null, or not an object), 2 `paused` (a `pauses[]` entry with `stops_builds:true`, or `pool_cap` 0), 3 `saturated` (free_slots 0, ready > 0), 4 `busy` (running > 0, or ready > 0 with free slots), 5 `blocked` (ready 0, blocked+held+parked > 0), 6 `starved` (ready 0, nothing blocked/held/parked, free slots), else `unknown` `no verdict rule matched`. Nulls in rules 5/6 print `?` and add `; unmeasured: <names>`. Verified by `tests/utilization-verdict.bh-3988-test.sh` (every rule through the real collector, plus the why strings) and `tests/utilization-tickets.bh-3988-test.sh` (stub `gh`/curl/fleet-conf, T1–T14), both run with `HARNESS_TEST_SANDBOX=1`. Probe after deploy: `curl -s -H "authorization: Bearer $HARNESS_SECRET" "$JOBS_URL/api/utilization" \| jq -c '.rows[] \| [.project,.verdict,.payload.tickets]'` shows a `tickets` object on every row after the next :07 hub tick |
| Utilization screen (BH-4011 / BH-3988.3) | Nav UTIL (right after CI) → `/utilization`; a PROJECT cell → `/p/<project>` (URL-encoded) | One read through the coord proxy, polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/utilization` (the worker's `GET /api/utilization`, newest snapshot per project; no Pages Function of its own). The UTILIZATION section is a `SortableTable wide` with one row per fleet.conf project (`useFleet()` minus `hub`/`fleet`): PROJECT `Link`, VERDICT (a `button.btn` with `aria-expanded` and `title` = why, holding a `span.tag` — `tag--warn` for starved/blocked/paused, `tag--plain` for busy/saturated, `tag--idle` for unknown/no snapshot — plus a `stale` tag when `as_of` is over 2h old or unparseable), READY, BLOCKED, HELD (titled "capped at 30 reads, may be low" when `held_complete:false`), PARKED, RUNNING, CAP, FREE (an integer or `unlimited`), PAUSES (`name · <since age>` per pause), PRS REVIEW, PRS CI, PRS MQ, AS OF (age, ISO in the title). Default sort is VERDICT ascending by severity: starved, blocked, paused, unknown, no snapshot, saturated, busy. A bucket that is null, absent, a string or negative is `—`, never 0; with today's tracer payload (builds section only) every tickets/pauses/prs column is `—` and the verdict is `unknown`. A fleet project with no snapshot is the row `no snapshot`. States: Skeleton until the read answers; a failed read (404 while the route is undeployed, 503 `schema not applied`) or a body without a `rows` array is the ErrorBox with RETRY; zero snapshot rows is the Empty "no utilization snapshot yet" / "the hourly lane writes the first one at :07" (never a table of `no snapshot` rows). Footnotes: "fleet list unread" (rows fall back to the snapshot projects), "not in fleet.conf, not shown: <names>", "<n> rows older than 2h". Clicking a VERDICT opens PAYLOAD · <project> below the table: the pauses as a `SortableTable` (KIND, NAME, SINCE, STOPS BUILDS; empty "no pauses"; "pauses unmeasured" when the payload has no pauses array), the basis as `bucket: value` lines, and the raw payload `<pre>` in its own `overflow-x:auto`; a `no snapshot` row says "no snapshot for <project> yet"; `payload_error` shows "payload unparseable". Precondition for real rows: the first `:07` hub tick of `bin/utilization-push.sh`. Verified by `console-ui/tests/utilization.bh-3988.test.js` and `tests/utilization-console.bh-3988-test.sh` |
| Utilization snapshot pauses and prs sections (BH-4010 / BH-3988 slice 3: `payload.pauses` and `payload.prs` of each `GET /api/utilization` row) | none (no UI; the console screen is a BH-3988 sibling); read-only sections sourced by `bin/utilization-push.sh` (hub, `7 * * * *`). `bin/lib/utilization/section-pauses.sh` `util_section_pauses <p>` lists `{kind,name,since,stops_builds}`: `flag` entries from `flags/paused-<p>` (true), `flags/all-off` (true) and `flags/automerge-disabled` (false) with `since` = file mtime; `pause` entries from `GET /api/pauses?active=1` rows scoped `fleet` or `<p>` (`name` `<scope>/<what>`, true only for what=builds\|triage); `hold` entries from `GET /api/conductor/held?limit=500` (`held_pages_whole`) rows on `bin/fleet-conf.sh <p> repo` (`name` `<repo>#<n> <kind>`, false). | Mission Control CONTROLS → Projects (BH-4082, slice C0 of BH-3979.8: the project registry, one row per project with the same columns as `fleet.conf`, edited through the generic CONTROLS editor) | [mission-control-settings.md](mission-control-settings.md) | Nav SETTINGS → the CONTROLS link row → "Projects" (`a.tab[href="/controls/projects"]`) → `/controls/projects` (`console-ui/src/screens/Controls.js` over the `projects` entry of `CONTROL_KINDS` in `console-ui/src/lib/controls.js`). Reads `GET /api/coord/projects` (worker `GET /api/projects`, `worker/jobs/src/routes/projects.js`, `{projects, as_of}` ordered by project) and writes one row with `PUT /api/coord/projects` (allowlist row in `console-ui/functions/api/coord/[[path]].js`: project, repo, prod_url, board_api_prefix, cron_offset, box, tailnet_ip, note, reason; reason required, the proxy stamps the actor). The worker validates every column and answers 400 `{error}` with nothing written: project lowercased once and `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$`, repo an `owner/name` slug, prod_url `https://…` or blank/`-` (stored NULL), board API path `/…` or blank/`-` (NULL), cron_offset a whole number 0-9 (refused, never clamped), box a lowercase slug or `local` or blank, tailnet address a dotted IPv4 or blank, no newline in any of those, note at most 512 characters. Each change batches one `config_changes` row (kind `project`, name = the project) with the upsert; an unchanged re-save writes none. There is no DELETE (405 at the proxy, unrouted at the worker). The table is `projects` in the jobs D1 (`worker/jobs/schema.sql`, self-created from `CREATED_TABLES`); it ships EMPTY and NOTHING in `bin/` reads it yet, so editing a row does not change `fleet.conf` on any box (the page's explainer says so). Selectors and states are the CONTROLS editor's (see the BH-2513 row): `th[aria-sort] button` per field, the identity cell links to `/config/project/<project>` (its change history), EDIT/NEW open the in-app Modal with a `type=number` triage-minute input, zero rows is "no Projects rows on record yet". Suites: `worker/jobs/test/routes/projects.bh-4082.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/coord-projects-allowlist.bh-4082.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/controls-projects.bh-4082.test.js`, `tests/projects-control.bh-4082-test.sh` (no cron caller — an operator drives it) |

| A protected setting's signature carries a nonce, so an old signed value can't be replayed (BH-3905, built as BH-4087). `PUT /api/settings` of `human_verdict_mode`, `human_verdict_sig_mode`, any `lead_authority.*` or any `risk_tier.*` key, once the existing PUT rules accept it, appends a row to the append-only `setting_sigs` table, `(key, seq, sig)` with sig = HMAC-SHA256(`HUMAN_VERDICT_SIG_KEY`, `bh-setting-v2\n<key>\n<value>\n<seq>`), in the value's own batch, and answers `signed: true, sig_seq: <n>`; a seq another PUT took first answers 409 `concurrent protected write, retry` and writes nothing; with the key unset it answers `signed: false` and writes no row. The two switches still get their v1 `<key>.sig` row. `protectedMode` judges the key's newest `setting_sigs` row by seq: it verifies the value, `shadow`; it does not, or the key is unset, `enforce` with the BH-3846 mismatch log. A key with no row falls back to the v1 `.sig` rules and, when the v1 sig verifies, logs `BH-3905 <key> carries a v1 signature with no nonce`. A D1 writer with DDL can still drop the triggers and delete newer rows; the `HUMAN_VERDICT_ENFORCE=1` floor is still what closes that. | The `lead_authority.*` and `risk_tier.*` rows are behind the setting signature (BH-3905, built as BH-4090). A bearer `PUT /api/settings` that LOOSENS one needs `x-human-verdict-token`, in shadow and enforce alike: authority may go to `human` from anything, to `human:precision` unless current is `human`, to `lane` or anything else only from absent, `lane` or `human:precision` (whitespace removed first); `risk_tier.*` only to a whitespace-split superset of the current globs. Otherwise 403 `<key> <from> -> <to> loosens it and needs the human verdict token (x-human-verdict-token); use bin/protected-setting.sh` with `key`, `from`, `to` (and `removed` for `risk_tier.*`), and nothing is written. "Current" is the effective value: under `human_verdict_sig_mode` enforce (or an unreadable mode) a row whose newest `setting_sigs` signature does not verify, a row deleted after it was signed, or a row written with no signing key reads, on `GET /api/settings/<key>`, as `{value:"human", unproven:true, stored_value}` for authority (`la_decide` rc 1) and as 409 `<key> signature does not verify; re-PUT it with the human token` for `risk_tier.*` (`setting_get` rc 9); in shadow it reads as stored with a `[settings] BH-3905 unproven <key>` warn. A key never set and never signed still reads `setting: null`. The bulk `GET /api/settings` adds `proven: true|false` (null when `setting_sigs` is unreadable) on those rows only and never rewrites them. `bin/protected-setting.sh <key> <value> [note]` PUTs with the human token from `HUMAN_VERDICT_TOKEN_FILE` through a mode-600 header file, prints `signed=` / `sig_seq=`, and is rc 1 on a non-2xx or an unsigned write. First paint: an inline `<script>` that `themeBootPlugin` (console-ui/src/theme/boot.js, wired in console-ui/vite.config.js) puts straight after `<meta name="theme-color">` in dist/index.html, before every `<link>`; it reads `ui.theme`, then the `ui.theme.fleet` cache, and sets every token, `data-theme` on `<html>` and both metas. console-ui/src/main.js runs `syncFleetDefault` once after render. Themes in console-ui/src/theme/themes.js (+ phosphor.js), order in resolve.js, painting and storage in apply.js. SAVE is `PUT /api/coord/settings {key: ui.theme.custom.<slug>, value: <theme JSON>}`; the coord proxy (console-ui/functions/api/coord/[[path]].js) validates it and stamps the actor, the worker caps it at 4096 and requires a JSON object. Saved themes render as `section.theme-card[data-theme-id="custom.<slug>"]` with "saved by <actor>", `button[data-theme-edit]`, `[data-theme-export]` (downloads `<slug>.theme.json`) and `[data-theme-delete]` (in-app confirm, then `DELETE /api/coord/settings/ui.theme.custom.<slug>`, audited in config_changes). `input[type=file][data-theme-import]` runs the same validator; a bad file shows `[data-import-errors]`. `button[data-fleet-pick]` on every card writes `ui.theme.default` and shows only when `GET /api/coord/whoami` says `access: on`. A fleet row naming a deleted custom theme reads `Fleet default: Mill (the settings row names custom.<slug>, and no saved theme has that id)` and the page paints Mill. First paint of a custom theme comes from localStorage `ui.theme.cache` (console-ui/src/theme/boot.js). One read through the coord proxy, polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/ledger?kind=main_red_attrib&limit=200`. A counter strip of `.stat` tiles (TOTAL · RISK TIER high/low/unknown · LANE scoped/serial/unknown · MAP GAPS, the rows with status `gap`) over a `SortableTable wide` in its own `.tablewrap` (scrolls inside its container at 375px): CULPRIT `#<pr>` linking `https://github.com/<slug>/pull/<pr>` (`-` with no pr), REVERT linking the revert PR or the text `none`, MAP GAP linking the gap issue or the status word, LANE, RISK TIER, MISSED SUITES, WHEN (`whenLabel`, sorted on the raw `updated_at`, newest first by default); every header a button with `aria-sort`. A detail that is missing or not JSON renders its cells `-`. Skeleton until the read answers; a failed read (including a failed poll over earlier data) is the ErrorBox "could not read the main-red reverts" with no counter strip; zero rows is the Empty "No main-red reverts recorded."; exactly 200 rows adds the note "newest 200 shown; counters cover these only" |

| Merge-queue stages: waiting line and per-PR stage track (BH-5669, BH-5008 child 3) | `/merge-queue` (and `/merge-queue?project=<name>`): under the batch section, the section `WAITING TO BE BATCHED` is a `SortableTable` (`.tablewrap table.tbl`) with the columns POSITION, PR, REPO, QUEUED AT, sorted by position by default. Each PR cell is an `<a href="https://github.com/<repo>/pull/<n>">`, plus a `/t/<key>` link when a `pr_owners` row maps the PR to a ticket. The section renders nothing when nobody waits, an error box on a load failure, and a "List may be incomplete" note on `complete:false`. `/jobs` and `/t/<key>`: in the JobsTable TICKET cell, a compact six-station track (`.track-scroll .track[aria-label]`, stations Queued, In batch, Checks, Merging, Merged, Deploy verified), then one line per failure, each an `<a>` to the check run or the draft: `Failing: <check>`, `Splitting: testing #a, #b, step s of S`, `Dequeued: <check> failed on #n alone` | `GET /api/coord/merge-queue/live[?project=]` (`worker/jobs/src/routes/merge-queue-live.js` → `applyStages` in `src/lib/merge-queue-live-stages.js`) sets each batch's `stage`, `failing{name,url}` and `bisect{step,steps,parent}`, `prs["<repo>#<n>"] = {stage, detail, batch, at, key, project}` from each PR's newest `pr_state_events` `state='queue'` row of the last 24 h, and `waiting[{repo, pr, position, at, key}]` from the `queued (<n>)` rows, by position. The rows come from the signed github webhook through `stageStatements` (`src/lib/merge-queue-intake-stages.js`): `batched` on draft open, `checking` on a check, `merging` on a green check suite when every stored check of the draft is completed and none failed, no `checking`/`merging` on a closed batch, `merged` on a close with `merged === true`, `dequeued` on a failed check of a 1-member bisect draft. A bisect draft (members a strict subset of a failed batch's) gets `bisect_parent/step/steps` on `merge_queue_batches`. The intake never writes `queued` or `deploy_verified`; until a writer for `queued (<n>)` rows lands, the waiting line is empty. Preconditions: the repo is armed (`projects` row or `merge_queue_repos`); a ticket-key track needs a `pr_owners` row for the PR. Every track on a page shares one request per 5 s. Verified by `worker/jobs/test/merge-queue-stages.bh-5669.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/pr-stage-track.bh-5669.test.js` and `console-ui/tests/merge-queue-waiting.bh-5669.test.js` |

| Risk tier sign-off of low-risk PR heads (BH-5022, BH-4973.1) | Mission Control: `/merge-queue` RISK TIERS section (`?tier=low\|medium\|high`), and the RISK TIER fact on the PR screen (see the BH-3158 and BH-1952 rows) | hub lane `bin/lead-review-enqueue.sh tick` (`*/5`, `cron/lanes/713-lead-review-enqueue.cron`) classifies every builder-approved head with no standing verdict through `bin/lib/risk-tier.sh` (`rt_mode`, `rt_classify`) when the settings row `risk_tier.mode.<owner>.<name>` (else `risk_tier.mode`; none = off) is `shadow` or `enforce`; wave 1 honours those rows for `example-org/cromford` only (`RT_MODE_REPOS`), every other repo is off. Seeded `risk_tier.mode.example-org.cromford = enforce` in `worker/jobs/schema.sql`. Rules rows (fallback constants in the lib): `risk_tier.high_globs`, `risk_tier.sensitive_globs`, `risk_tier.low_globs`, `risk_tier.high_lines` 600, `risk_tier.high_files` 25, `risk_tier.low_lines` 300; the lead's protected globs are always high. Wave 1 low is ONLY a diff under 300 lines whose every path is under `docs/`, `console-ui/src/screens/` or `console-ui/src/ui/` (`RT_LOW_CEILING`), or is a NEW test file outside `tests/lib/` (an added `tests/<name>-test.sh`, `tests/mc/<name>-test.sh` or `console-ui/tests/<name>.test.js`, `RT_LOW_NEW_TESTS`); both are constants a row can narrow and never widen. Any edit to an existing test file, even a purely additive one, any test helper or fixture, any `prompts/` file and any `bin/` file is never low. Low + enforce = `bin/lead-verdict.sh post <slug> <n> <head> MERGE --source risk-tier --note "low risk: <reasons>"` (a STANDING `risk-tier` verdict row and a `lead-verdict` success status), no lead request; a withheld sign-off (protected path, authority not the lane's or unreadable) exits 5 with NO row written and is requested with `priority: 1`; high = a lead request with `priority: 1`, and a re-confirm with `priority: 1` sets a still-`requested` row to priority 1 (BH-5151's re-order; a re-confirm without a priority never changes it); shadow logs one `WOULD sign off` line. Every classification is `POST /api/risk-tiers` (read back by `GET /api/risk-tiers?repo=&pr=&tier=&limit=`) and a line in `state/lead-review-enqueue/risk.tsv` keyed on a checksum of the rules rows (a changed row reclassifies every cached head; a withheld sign-off is a `withheld` line, so later ticks do not post it again); the tick line adds `risk_low= risk_signed= risk_shadow= risk_medium= risk_high=`. `bin/lead-review.sh` supersedes a request the enqueue filed (`requested_by` `lead-review-enqueue:*`) whose head carries a standing risk-tier MERGE with no model call, and reviews one a person filed; `to_lane_lead_fence` never clears a lead-authored PR on one. Verified by `tests/risk-tier.bh-5022-test.sh`, `tests/lead-review-enqueue-risk.bh-5022-test.sh`, `tests/lead-verdict-risk-tier.bh-5022-test.sh`, `tests/risk-tiers-console.bh-5022-test.sh`, `worker/jobs/test/risk-tiers.bh-5022.test.js` and `console-ui/tests/risk-tiers.bh-5022.test.js` |

Live: hub `logs/fly-pool.log` lines `TOKEN-401 <KEY>: … requeued, deferrals unchanged (N), <machine> destroyed`, `<machine>: its GitHub token answered 401 — destroying instead of reusing` and `<KEY>: hotfix deferred on a reused machine last time — left for a fresh spawn`; `"fresh_spawn":true` in `state/build-queue/<p>/<KEY>.json`. What usually lies: a token-401 deferral on a FRESH machine (no `reused_from`) means the hub's token mint is broken, not a stale machine. | Participant machines in Mission Control: approve, deny and revoke enrollment, browse enrolled machines and their claims (BH-5353) | [../notes/2026-10-08-BH-5353-participants-screen.md](../notes/2026-10-08-BH-5353-participants-screen.md) | Nav FLEET → MACHINES → SEE PARTICIPANTS (`/machines/participants`); a NAME cell opens `/machines/participants/<id>`. Selectors: `[data-testid=participant-pending]` (one card per pending row, a checkbox per requested role, buttons APPROVE and DENY; APPROVE is disabled with every role unticked), the ENROLLED table's NAME links (`a[href^="/machines/participants/"]`), LAST RESULT links (`/pr/<project>/<n>`), the detail's REVOKE (approved rows only), and the confirm dialog's `.modal__actions` buttons (CANCEL, APPROVE / DENY / REVOKE). States: no rows → `no machines enrolled`; a failed load → `could not load` with RETRY; an unknown id → `no such participant`; a refused decision → a warn banner with the worker's text. Routes `GET /api/coord/participants`, `GET /api/coord/participants/<id>`, `POST /api/coord/participants/<uuid>/approve` (`{granted_roles}`), `…/deny`, `…/revoke`. Preconditions: one pending and one approved row in `participant_machines` (a machine enrolls with `POST /api/participants/request`); `LEAD_VERDICT_TOKEN` set on the console Pages project, else every decision answers 503 naming it. Tests `console-ui/tests/participants.bh-5353.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/participants-proxy.bh-5353.test.js`, `worker/jobs/test/participants-detail.bh-5353.test.js`, `tests/participants-console.bh-5353-test.sh` |
| Participant machines: a machine outside the Fly pool requests to join, the lead approves, denies or revokes it, and its token passes the role check (BH-5349) | [../MERGE-CONDUCTOR.md](../MERGE-CONDUCTOR.md) | `worker/jobs/src/routes/participants.js`, registered after `...poolState` in `worker/jobs/src/index.js`, exports `PARTICIPANT_ROLES` (build, fix, lead-review) and `participantAuth(req, env, role)`. Every route sits behind the `HARNESS_SECRET` bearer. `POST /api/participants/request`: `name` is trimmed and lowercased, a taken name or a reused `token_sha256` is 409, and the row is always `pending`. `GET /api/participants?status=` lists newest first, at most 200 rows, with `truncated`. `GET /api/participants/whoami` needs `x-participant-token`: blank is 401, unknown/pending/denied/revoked is 403. `POST …/:id/approve` (lead token, `granted_roles` must be a subset of `requested_roles`), `…/deny` and `…/revoke` are CAS-guarded, and a repeat answers `unchanged: true`. No answer carries `token_sha256`. Table `participant_machines` comes from `worker/jobs/schema.sql` and is self-created from `CREATED_TABLES`. `lead_review_requests.heartbeat_at/participant/head_authors` arrive by `ensureColumns`. `cost_rows.participant` and the settings row `lead_review_claim_stale_s`=900 are seeded with no reader yet. Verified by `worker/jobs/test/participants.bh-5349.test.js` (real SQLite through `worker.fetch`), `worker/jobs/test/participants-schema.bh-5349.test.js` and `worker/jobs/test/route-table.test.js`, via `cd worker/jobs && npm test` |
| Lead review claims are held by a heartbeat, taken back when stale, claimed by enrolled participants, and refused to the head's own authors (BH-5351) | [../notes/2026-10-09-BH-5351-participant-lead-review-claims.md](../notes/2026-10-09-BH-5351-participant-lead-review-claims.md) | `worker/jobs/src/routes/lead-reviews.js`: `POST /api/lead-reviews/:id/heartbeat {job_id}` (bearer; the holder only, else 409); `POST …/:id/claim` takes a running row quiet past settings `lead_review_claim_stale_s` (fallback 900) and answers `reclaimed_from`; with `x-participant-token` the claim goes through `participantAuth(…, 'lead-review')` (401/403 naming why) and binds `participant`, `box=participant:<name>`; a claimer in `head_authors` is 409 `self-review refused` (a participant on a row with no `head_authors`: `authorship unknown`; a box only with settings `lead_review_sod_boxes=1`), one `stage_events` row per refusal; `POST …/start` stamps `heartbeat_at` and refuses the participant header. | Merge-queue batch runs only the suites whose input fingerprint changed; CI records every shard's results in the suite ledger (BH-5354.2 / BH-5368) | [2026-10-08-BH-5354-suite-reuse.md](../notes/2026-10-08-BH-5354-suite-reuse.md) | `bin/ci-plan.sh` reuse step after the tier step: a batch (not escalated by the `full` label, `unmapped:` or `member labels unreadable`) or a push-to-main corpus with `CI_PLAN_REUSE=1` runs `bin/suite-reuse.sh plan --class ci` and prints `reuse: reused <suite> from <run_id> (<d>s) fp <64-hex>` (BH-6406: the fp is the suite's row in the run's `fingerprints.tsv`; a shard reads the `fingerprints.tsv` beside `CI_PLAN_REUSED_FILE` and prints `fp -` without one) then `reuse: on — ran X reused R of N (saved ~Ss)` (or `reuse: off: <why>`), a `::notice title=ci-plan reuse::` and the step-summary line; outputs `reused`, `saved_s`, `reuse`; a shard subtracts `CI_PLAN_REUSED_FILE` with no ledger call (unreadable = rc 2); a quarantined suite is never reused; `--tier nightly` or `CI_EVENT=schedule` ignores `CI_PLAN_REUSED_FILE` and prints `reuse: off: nightly never reuses` (BH-6406). `.github/workflows/harness-ci.yml`: plan step env `CI_PLAN_REUSE`, `CI_REUSE_CTX` (`join(fromJSON(vars.CI_RUNNER …), ':')`), `SUITE_LEDGER_URL` (repo variable), `SUITE_LEDGER_TOKEN` (secret, push / merge-queue only), artifact `suite-reuse-plan` (`fingerprints.tsv`, `reused.tsv`); tests-shard downloads it, tees the trace to `$RUNNER_TEMP/gate.log` under `set -o pipefail`, and step `record suite results` (`if: always()`, the token in that step only) runs `bin/suite-reuse.sh record --from-gate-log`, run key `<run_id>-<attempt>-<shard>`, shard 0 with `--reused`; `decisions` prints `reuse: <state> (reused R, saved ~Ss)` under the plan summary. `.github/workflows/harness-nightly.yml`: step `fingerprint tonight's suites` and a record-only `record suite results` (scheduled runs, never `plan`), whose `suite-reuse: MISS` warnings are the impact-map signal. Builder rows never satisfy CI: the plan asks with `--class ci` and the ledger answers a `x-suite-ledger-token` lookup with `class = 'ci'` rows only. | Suite reuse saved minutes (BH-5369, BH-5354.3) | Nav CI → /ci, the SUITE REUSE section directly under CI HEALTH | One read through the coord proxy, polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/suite-results/saved?days=14` (the jobs worker's bearer-only `GET /api/suite-results/saved`, grouped from `suite_reuse_runs` by UTC day, repo and class; `days` clamps to 1..60, a non-integer is a 400). Two `.stat` tiles: CI MIN SAVED TODAY and CI MIN SAVED 7D, class `ci` only; a footnote names the builder minutes for 7 days apart. A `SortableTable wide` of DAY (a `button[aria-expanded]`), REPO, RUNNER (ci/builder), RUNS, RAN, REUSED, SAVED min, default DAY descending. Clicking DAY opens RUNS · <day> · <repo> · <runner> from `GET /api/coord/suite-results/runs?day=YYYY-MM-DD&repo=&class=` (newest first, at most 200, a "first 200 runs only" note when capped; empty "no runs on <day>"; a failed read is the ErrorBox with RETRY). RUN is `a[href^="https://github.com/"][target=_blank]` only for a ci run key `<run_id>-<attempt>-<shard>`, plain text otherwise; its SUITES `button[aria-expanded]` opens REUSED · <run_key> (SUITE, FROM RUN, SAVED s) or the note "the ledger did not record which suites this run reused (<n> reused)" while `suite_reuse_runs` has no `reused_suites` column. Zero rows is the Empty "no reuse recorded yet"; a 404 from the worker adds "the suite-reuse routes are not deployed on the jobs worker yet"; any other failure is the ErrorBox with RETRY. Tables scroll inside `.tablewrap`. Tests: `console-ui/tests/suite-reuse.bh-5354.test.js`, `worker/jobs/test/suite-reuse-saved.bh-5354.test.js`, `tests/suite-reuse-saved.bh-5369-test.sh` |
| Queue waste ledger and QUEUE WASTE section (BH-5374 / BH-5356.2) | Nav CI → /ci; QUEUE WASTE is its own section right under CI HEALTH | One read through the coord proxy, polled every 60s: `GET /api/coord/queue-batch-minutes/daily?days=7` (worker `worker/jobs/src/routes/queue-batch-minutes.js`, table `queue_batch_minutes` in `worker/jobs/schema.sql` applied by `bin/harness-sync.sh`, else 503 `queue_batch_minutes schema not applied`). Two `.stat` tiles, WASTED TODAY and WASTED 7D, in min: the non-merged (`discarded_min`) minutes of today (UTC) and of the window; merged minutes never count. A `SortableTable wide` (DAY as a button with `aria-expanded`, DISCARDED min, REUSED min shown as `span.na` "—" titled `no reuse record` when #5368's reuse record is absent, RED min, RESETS), newest day first. Clicking a DAY opens DRAFTS · <day>: a `SortableTable` of that day's draft runs (DRAFT `#n` linking to `https://github.com/<repo>/pull/<n>`, RUN linking to `https://github.com/<repo>/actions/runs/<id>`, OUTCOME merged/reset/red/bisect/closed, MIN, REUSED, SOURCE billable/jobs). Skeleton until the read answers; a failed read or a payload of the wrong shape ("unreadable queue-waste payload") is the ErrorBox with RETRY, never zeros; a good read with no rows is the Empty "no queue waste recorded yet"; `truncated` shows "first 2000 rows only, totals may be low". Rows are written by `bmin_tick` in `bin/lib/mc-batch-minutes.sh`, called from `bin/merge-conductor.sh` after `bres_tick` on opted-in Mergify repos; each row logs `QUEUE_MINUTES draft=<n> outcome=<o> minutes=<m> reused=<r>` in `logs/merge-conductor.log`. Verified by `tests/queue-batch-minutes.bh-5374-test.sh`, `worker/jobs/test/queue-batch-minutes.bh-5374.test.js` and `console-ui/tests/queue-waste.bh-5374.test.js`; probe: `curl -s -H "authorization: Bearer $HARNESS_SECRET" "$JOBS_URL/api/queue-batch-minutes/daily?days=7" \| jq '.days[0]'` |

| Live merge-queue intake and `GET /api/merge-queue/live` (BH-5667, BH-5008 child 1) | No screen yet: `GET /api/coord/merge-queue/live[?project=<name or owner/repo>]` through the console origin answers `{batches, waiting, prs, as_of, complete, window_s, window_source}`. The seams `MergeQueueWaiting` (`/merge-queue`, the waiting line) and `PrStageTrack` (the `/jobs` and `/t/<key>` TICKET cell, the per-PR stage track) are filled by BH-5669 (see the merge-queue stages row); `MergeQueueBatches` and `MergeQueueStrip` are filled by BH-5668 (see the batch progress and ETA row) | the github webhook receiver `POST /webhooks/github` (`worker/jobs/src/routes/webhooks.js`). A signed `pull_request` (`opened`/`reopened`/`synchronize`/`edited`/`closed`) or `check_run` (`created`/`completed`/`rerequested`/`requested_action`) delivery whose ref is `mergify/merge-queue/<suffix>` (`isMergeQueueRef`) upserts `merge_queue_batches` (members = the `#<n>` refs of the draft body) and `merge_queue_checks` in the same D1 batch as its `webhook_deliveries` row (`src/lib/merge-queue-intake.js`). Inert until armed: the repo must be in a `projects` row or in the comma list of the settings row `merge_queue_repos`. A queue-only delivery answers `200 {queue:{rows}}`; a wake-bearing one adds `queue` to its wake answer. Outcome is `merged` only on `pull_request.merged === true`, else `failed` when a check row failed, else `closed`. The live route reads D1 only (no GitHub or Mergify call, `cache-control: max-age=4`), keeps a finished batch for the settings row `merge_queue_live_window_s` seconds (default 900), and answers `complete:false` past 50 batches or 3000 check rows. Stage rows (`pr_state_events` state `queue`, `queueStageStmt`) are skipped by `GET /api/pr-state`, the digest's current-state read and the sfe tick (`not_state=queue`). Verified by `worker/jobs/test/merge-queue-intake.bh-5667.test.js`, `console-ui/tests/merge-queue-live-data.bh-5667.test.js` and `tests/pr-state-queue-rows.bh-5667-test.sh` |
